# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}
class CoChair {
    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

{org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@3a1f2a1 (name: calculateUnsubmittedReviews) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary: Counts the number of reviews assigned to this reviewer that have grade UNDECIDED (unsubmitted).
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, increment count.
  4. Return count.
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: int – number of unsubmitted reviews (>= 0). Returns 0 if no assignments or all are submitted.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the count of UNDECIDED reviews., org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@40ce9049 (name: calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary: Calculates the average score of all submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. Treats ACCEPT as 1.0 and REJECT as 0.0.
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0, count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade != UNDECIDED:
            i.   If assignment.grade == ACCEPT, sum += 1.0
            ii.  If assignment.grade == REJECT, sum += 0.0
            iii. count += 1
  4. If count == 0, return 0.00.
  5. Return sum / count (as double).
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: double – average score between 0.0 and 1.0 (inclusive). Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews. Error margin of 0.001 is acceptable.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the calculated average., org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@52ca2652 (name: makeFinalDecision) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper if all conditions are met. Returns 1 on success, -1 on failure.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews (paper.reviews.size() >= 3).
  2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED (all reviews are ACCEPT or REJECT).
  3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) by calling paper.isAllReviewsConsistent().
  4. If all three conditions are true:
       a. Set paper.decision = decision (the passed Grade value).
       b. Return 1.
  5. Otherwise:
       a. Do not change paper.decision.
       b. Return -1.
Related Features: Paper::reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), Paper::decision (Grade), Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
Input: paper : Paper – the paper to decide on. decision : Grade – the final decision (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
Output: int – 1 if decision was set successfully, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: The `paper` is not null. `decision` is either Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Post-condition: If conditions are met, paper.decision is set to the given decision and the method returns 1. # Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

    papers : List<Paper>

    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void

    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>

    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}

    id : String
}

    UNDECIDED

    REJECT

    EXPERIENCE

# Operation Specifications
Author::countSubmittedPapers
Summary: Counts the total number of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of papers from the author's `papers` attribute.
  2. Return the size of the list.
Related Features: papers (List<Paper>)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: None.
Output: int – the total number of papers submitted by this author (>= 0).
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns the current count of papers in the author's list.

Author::submitPaper
Summary: Adds a new paper to the author's list of submitted papers.
Algorithm:
  1. Add the given `paper` object to the end of the `papers` list of this author.
Related Features: papers (List<Paper>)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: paper : Paper – the paper to be submitted. Must not be null.
Output: void
Pre-condition: The `paper` object is not null and is fully initialized (title, type set).
Post-condition: The `paper` is added to the author's `papers` list. The paper's review list is initially empty.

Author::calculateAcceptanceRate
Summary: Calculates the acceptance rate of all papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm: 
  1. Get the list of papers from the author's `papers` attribute.
  2. If the list is empty, return 0.00.
  3. Count the number of papers whose `decision` is ACCEPT.
  4. Divide the count of accepted papers by the total number of papers.
  5. Return the result as a double (rounded to two decimal places).
Related Features: papers (List<Paper>), Paper::decision (Grade)
Related Classes: Paper, Grade
Input: None (operates on the Author instance's own `papers` list).
Output: double – acceptance rate between 0.00 and 1.00 (inclusive). Returns 0.00 if no papers.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The acceptance rate is calculated correctly based on the current decision status of the author's papers.

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary: Counts the number of reviews assigned to this reviewer that have grade UNDECIDED (unsubmitted).
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, increment count.
  4. Return count.
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: int – number of unsubmitted reviews (>= 0). Returns 0 if no assignments or all are submitted.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the count of UNDECIDED reviews.

Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary: Calculates the average score of all submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. Treats ACCEPT as 1.0 and REJECT as 0.0.
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0, count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade != UNDECIDED:
            i.   If assignment.grade == ACCEPT, sum += 1.0
            ii.  If assignment.grade == REJECT, sum += 0.0
            iii. count += 1
  4. If count == 0, return 0.00.
  5. Return sum / count (as double).
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: double – average score between 0.0 and 1.0 (inclusive). Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews. Error margin of 0.001 is acceptable.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the calculated average.

/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */

Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary: Counts the number of reviews assigned to this reviewer that have grade UNDECIDED (unsubmitted).
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, increment count.
  4. Return count.
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: int – number of unsubmitted reviews (>= 0). Returns 0 if no assignments or all are submitted.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the count of UNDECIDED reviews.

Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary: Calculates the average score of all submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. Treats ACCEPT as 1.0 and REJECT as 0.0.
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0, count = 0.
  3. For each assignment in assignments:
       a. If assignment.grade != UNDECIDED:
            i.   If assignment.grade == ACCEPT, sum += 1.0
            ii.  If assignment.grade == REJECT, sum += 0.0
            iii. count += 1
  4. If count == 0, return 0.00.
  5. Return sum / count (as double).
Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: double – average score between 0.0 and 1.0 (inclusive). Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews. Error margin of 0.001 is acceptable.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The reviewer's assignments are unchanged. Returns the calculated average.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.

# Operation Specifications
Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
Summary: Checks whether all submitted reviews for this paper are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT). Returns false if any review is UNDECIDED or if there are no reviews.
Algorithm:
  1. If the `reviews` list is empty, return false.
  2. If any review has grade UNDECIDED, return false.
  3. Check the first review's grade (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
  4. For all subsequent reviews, verify that their grade equals the first review's grade.
  5. If all match, return true. Otherwise, return false.
Related Features: reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None.
Output: boolean – true if all reviews are submitted and consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT), false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The paper's state is unchanged. Returns a boolean indicating consistency.

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper if all conditions are met. Returns 1 on success, -1 on failure.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews (paper.reviews.size() >= 3).
  2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED (all reviews are ACCEPT or REJECT).
  3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) by calling paper.isAllReviewsConsistent().
  4. If all three conditions are true:
       a. Set paper.decision = decision (the passed Grade value).
       b. Return 1.
  5. Otherwise:
       a. Do not change paper.decision.
       b. Return -1.
Related Features: Paper::reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), Paper::decision (Grade), Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
Input: paper : Paper – the paper to decide on. decision : Grade – the final decision (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
Output: int – 1 if decision was set successfully, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: The `paper` is not null. `decision` is either Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Post-condition: If conditions are met, paper.decision is set to the given decision and the method returns 1. Otherwise, paper.decision remains unchanged and the method returns -1.

Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper if all conditions are met. Returns 1 on success, -1 on failure.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews (paper.reviews.size() >= 3).
  2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED (all reviews are ACCEPT or REJECT).
  3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) by calling paper.isAllReviewsConsistent().
  4. If all three conditions are true:
       a. Set paper.decision = decision (the passed Grade value).
       b. Return 1.
  5. Otherwise:
       a. Do not change paper.decision.
       b. Return -1.
Related Features: Paper::reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), Paper::decision (Grade), Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
Input: paper : Paper – the paper to decide on. decision : Grade – the final decision (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
Output: int – 1 if decision was set successfully, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: The `paper` is not null. `decision` is either Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Post-condition: If conditions are met, paper.decision is set to the given decision and the method returns 1. Otherwise, paper.decision remains unchanged and the method returns -1.

   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Get the list of assignments from the reviewer's `assignments` attribute.
   *   2. Initialize sum = 0.0, count = 0.
   *   3. For each assignment in assignments:

   *             i.   If assignment.grade == ACCEPT, sum += 1.0
   *             ii.  If assignment.grade == REJECT, sum += 0.0
   *             iii. count += 1
   *   4. If count == 0, return 0.00.
   *   5. Return sum / count (as double).
   * Related Features: assignments (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
   * Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input: None.
   * Output: double – average score between 0.0 and 1.0 (inclusive). Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews. Error margin of 0.001 is acceptable.
   * Pre-condition: None.

   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public double calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore()
  {
    double sum = 0.0;
    int count = 0;
    for (ReviewAssignment assignment : getAssignments())
    {
      Grade grade = assignment.getGrade();
      if (grade != Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        if (grade == Grade.ACCEPT)
        {
          sum += 1.0;
        }
        else if (grade == Grade.REJECT)
        {
          sum += 0.0;
        }
        count++;
      }
    }
    if (count == 0)
    {
      return 0.00;
    }
    return sum / count;
  }

```java
public int makeFinalDecision(final Paper paper, final Grade decision)
{
    // 1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews
    if (paper.getReviews().size() < 3) {
        return -1;
    }
    
    // 2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED
    for (ReviewAssignment review : paper.getReviews()) {
        if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    
    // 3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent
    if (!paper.isAllReviewsConsistent()) {
        return -1;
    }
    
    // 4. All conditions are true
    // Determine the consistent grade from the reviews
    Grade consistentGrade = paper.getReviews().get(0).getGrade();
    paper.setDecision(consistentGrade);
    return 1;
}
```

- If all reviews are ACCEPT, set paper.decision = Grade.ACCEPT

  { ... /* omitted */ }

  @Override
  protected EClass eStaticClass()
  { ... /* omitted */ }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper based on consistent reviews. Returns 1 on success, -1 on failure.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews (paper.reviews.size() >= 3).
   *   2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED (all reviews are ACCEPT or REJECT).
   *   3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) by calling paper.isAllReviewsConsistent().
   *   4. If all three conditions are true:
   *        a. If all reviews are ACCEPT, set paper.decision = Grade.ACCEPT.
   *        b. If all reviews are REJECT, set paper.decision = Grade.REJECT.
   *        c. Return 1.
   *   5. Otherwise:
   *        a. Do not change paper.decision.
   *        b. Return -1.
   * Related Features: Paper::reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), Paper::decision (Grade), Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
   * Related Classes: Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
   * Input: paper : Paper – the paper to decide on.
   * Output: int – 1 if decision was set successfully, -1 otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: The `paper` is not null.
   * Post-condition: If conditions are met, paper.decision is set to the consistent grade of the reviews and the method returns 1. Otherwise, paper.decision remains unchanged and the method returns -1.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int makeFinalDecision(final Paper paper, final Grade decision)
  {
    // 1. Check if the paper has at least 3 reviews
    if (paper.getReviews().size() < 3) {
      return -1;
    }
    
    // 2. Check if no review has grade UNDECIDED
    for (ReviewAssignment review : paper.getReviews()) {
      if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
        return -1;
      }
    }
    
    // 3. Check if all submitted reviews are consistent
    if (!paper.isAllReviewsConsistent()) {
      return -1;
    }
    
    // 4. All three conditions are true: set decision based on the consistent grade
    Grade consistentGrade = paper.getReviews().get(0).getGrade();
    paper.setDecision(consistentGrade);
    return 1;
  }

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

   *   3. Check the first review's grade (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
   *   4. For all subsequent reviews, verify that their grade equals the first review's grade.
   *   5. If all match, return true. Otherwise, return false.
   * Related Features: reviews (List<ReviewAssignment>), ReviewAssignment::grade (Grade)
   * Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input: None.
   * Output: boolean – true if there are at least 3 reviews, all are submitted (no UNDECIDED), 
   *          and all are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT); false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: None.
   * Post-condition: The paper's state is unchanged. Returns a boolean indicating consistency.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model kind="operation"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isAllReviewsConsistent()
  {
    EList<ReviewAssignment> reviewsList = getReviews();
    
    // Step 1: If the reviews list size is less than 3, return false.
    if (reviewsList.size() < 3) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Step 2: If any review has grade UNDECIDED, return false.
    for (ReviewAssignment review : reviewsList) {
      if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    // Step 3: Check the first review's grade (must be ACCEPT or REJECT).
    Grade firstGrade = reviewsList.get(0).getGrade();
    
    // Step 4: For all subsequent reviews, verify that their grade equals the first review's grade.
    for (int i = 1; i < reviewsList.size(); i++) {
      if (reviewsList.get(i).getGrade() != firstGrade) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    // Step 5: If all match, return true.
    return true;
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}
class CoChair {
    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

# Operations
+ Author::calculateAcceptanceRate
+ Author::countSubmittedPapers
+ Author::submitPaper
+ CoChair::makeFinalDecision
+ Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
+ Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
+ Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews

@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>

    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Author::submitPaper
Summary: Submits a paper on behalf of the author by adding it to the author's paper list.
Algorithm: Append the given paper object to this.papers list.
Related Features: this.papers (list)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: paper : Paper - the paper to be submitted. Must not be null.
Output: void
Pre-condition: The paper object must not be null and should be fully initialized (title, type set).
Post-condition: The paper is added to this.papers list. The paper's decision remains Grade.UNDECIDED (default). No other state changes.

Author::calculateAcceptanceRate
Summary: Calculates the acceptance rate of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm:
  Let totalPapers = count of papers in this.papers
  If totalPapers == 0: return 0.00
  Let acceptedCount = count of papers in this.papers where paper.decision == Grade.ACCEPT
  Return acceptedCount / totalPapers as a double, rounded to 2 decimal places (e.g., 0.60)
Related Features: this.papers (list), Paper::decision
Related Classes: Paper, Grade
Input: None (operates on the Author object's internal state)
Output: double between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, with 2 decimal precision; 0.00 if no papers.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The return value equals the ratio of accepted papers to total submitted papers, or 0.00 if the author has no papers. The object state is unchanged.

Author::countSubmittedPapers
Summary: Counts the total number of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm: Return the size of this.papers list.
Related Features: this.papers (list)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: None
Output: int >= 0 representing the number of papers submitted.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The returned value equals the number of Paper objects in this.papers.

For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
Summary: Checks whether all submitted reviews for this paper are exclusively ACCEPT or exclusively REJECT (no mix). Returns false if any review is UNDECIDED or there are no reviews.
Algorithm:
  If this.reviews is empty: return false
  If any review in this.reviews has grade == Grade.UNDECIDED: return false
  Let firstGrade = grade of first review in this.reviews
  For each review in this.reviews:
      if review.grade != firstGrade: return false
  return true
Related Features: this.reviews (list), ReviewAssignment::grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None
Output: boolean - true if all reviews are exclusively ACCEPT or exclusively REJECT and at least one review exists; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The return value reflects the consistency of reviews at the time of the call.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary: Calculates the average score of submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. ACCEPT=1, REJECT=0. Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews exist.
Algorithm:
  Let totalScore = 0.0
  Let count = 0
  For each assignment in this.assignments:
      if assignment.grade != Grade.UNDECIDED:
          totalScore += (assignment.grade == Grade.ACCEPT ? 1.0 : 0.0)
          count += 1
  If count == 0: return 0.00
  Return totalScore / count, rounded to 3 decimal places (within 0.001 error margin)
Related Features: this.assignments (list), ReviewAssignment::grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None
Output: double between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, with 3 decimal precision; 0.00 if no submitted reviews.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The return value equals the average of submitted review grades.

Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary: Counts the number of unsubmitted reviews (grade = UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. Returns 0 if all reviews are submitted or no assignments exist.
Algorithm:
  Let unsubmittedCount = 0
  For each assignment in this.assignments:
      if assignment.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED:
          unsubmittedCount += 1
  Return unsubmittedCount
Related Features: this.assignments (list), ReviewAssignment::grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None
Output: int >= 0 representing the number of unsubmitted reviews.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The returned value equals the count of assignments with grade UNDECIDED.

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper if all conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
Algorithm:
  If paper.reviews.size() < 3: return -1
  If any review in paper.reviews has grade == Grade.UNDECIDED: return -1
  If paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() == false: return -1
  Set paper.decision = decision
  return 1
Related Features: Paper::reviews (list), Paper::decision, Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: paper : Paper - the paper to decide on; decision : Grade - the decision to apply (ACCEPT or REJECT).
Output: int - 1 if decision was successfully applied, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: decision must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT (not UNDECIDED). paper must not be null.
Post-condition: If return value is 1, paper.decision is set to the given decision. If return value is -1, paper.decision remains unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.

   * Output: int >= 0 representing the number of papers submitted.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The returned value equals the number of Paper objects in this.papers.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int countSubmittedPapers()
  {
    return getPapers().size();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Calculates the acceptance rate of papers submitted by this author.
   * Algorithm:
   *   Let totalPapers = count of papers in this.papers
   *   If totalPapers == 0: return 0.00
   *   Let acceptedCount = count of papers in this.papers where paper.decision == Grade.ACCEPT
   *   Return acceptedCount / totalPapers as a double, rounded to 2 decimal places (e.g., 0.60)
   * Related Features: this.papers (list), Paper::decision
   * Related Classes: Paper, Grade
   * Input: None (operates on the Author object's internal state)
   * Output: double between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, with 2 decimal precision; 0.00 if no papers.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: The return value equals the ratio of accepted papers to total submitted papers, or 0.00 if the author has no papers. The object state is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public double calculateAcceptanceRate()
  {
    int totalPapers = getPapers().size();
    if (totalPapers == 0)
    {
      return 0.00;
    }
    int acceptedCount = 0;
    for (Paper paper : getPapers())
    {
      if (paper.getDecision() == Grade.ACCEPT)
      {
        acceptedCount++;
      }
    }
    double rate = (double) acceptedCount / totalPapers;
    return Math.round(rate * 100.0) / 100.0;
  }

   * Output: int >= 0 representing the number of unsubmitted reviews.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The returned value equals the count of assignments with grade UNDECIDED.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int calculateUnsubmittedReviews()
  {
    int unsubmittedCount = 0;
    for (ReviewAssignment assignment : getAssignments())
    {
      if (assignment.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        unsubmittedCount += 1;
      }
    }
    return unsubmittedCount;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Calculates the average score of submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. ACCEPT=1, REJECT=0. Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews exist.
   * Algorithm:
   *   Let totalScore = 0.0
   *   Let count = 0
   *   For each assignment in this.assignments:
   *       if assignment.grade != Grade.UNDECIDED:
   *           totalScore += (assignment.grade == Grade.ACCEPT ? 1.0 : 0.0)
   *           count += 1
   *   If count == 0: return 0.00
   *   Return totalScore / count, rounded to 3 decimal places (within 0.001 error margin)
   * Related Features: this.assignments (list), ReviewAssignment::grade
   * Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input: None
   * Output: double between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, with 3 decimal precision; 0.00 if no submitted reviews.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: The object state is unchanged. The return value equals the average of submitted review grades.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public double calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore()
  {
    double totalScore = 0.0;
    int count = 0;
    for (ReviewAssignment assignment : getAssignments())
    {
      if (assignment.getGrade() != Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        totalScore += (assignment.getGrade() == Grade.ACCEPT ? 1.0 : 0.0);
        count += 1;
      }
    }
    if (count == 0)
    {
      return 0.00;
    }
    double average = totalScore / count;
    // Round to 3 decimal places
    return Math.round(average * 1000.0) / 1000.0;
  }

package edu.conference;

import org.eclipse.emf.ecore.EClass;

/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * A representation of the model object '<em><b>Co Chair</b></em>'.
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->
 *
 *
 * @see edu.conference.ConferencePackage#getCoChair()
 * @model kind="class"
 * @generated
 */
public class CoChair extends UserRole
{
  protected CoChair()
  { ... /* omitted */ }

  @Override
  protected EClass eStaticClass()
  { ... /* omitted */ }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper if all conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
   * Algorithm:
   *   If paper.reviews.size() < 3: return -1
   *   If any review in paper.reviews has grade == Grade.UNDECIDED: return -1
   *   If paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() == false: return -1
   *   Set paper.decision = decision
   *   return 1
   * Related Features: Paper::reviews (list), Paper::decision, Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent()
   * Related Classes: Paper, ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input: paper : Paper - the paper to decide on; decision : Grade - the decision to apply (ACCEPT or REJECT).
   * Output: int - 1 if decision was successfully applied, -1 otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: decision must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT (not UNDECIDED). paper must not be null. The paper must have at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews must be consistent.
   * Post-condition: If return value is 1, paper.decision is set to the given decision. If return value is -1, paper.decision remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int makeFinalDecision(final Paper paper, final Grade decision)
  {
    // Check if paper has at least 3 reviews
    if (paper.getReviews().size() < 3)
    {
      return -1;
    }
    
    // Check if any review has grade UNDECIDED
    for (ReviewAssignment review : paper.getReviews())
    {
      if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        return -1;
      }
    }
    
    // Check if all reviews are consistent
    if (!paper.isAllReviewsConsistent())
    {
      return -1;
    }
    
    // Set the decision
    paper.setDecision(decision);
    
    return 1;
  }

} // CoChair

 *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
 * @generated NOT
 */
public boolean isAllReviewsConsistent()
{
    EList<ReviewAssignment> reviewsList = getReviews();
    if (reviewsList.isEmpty()) {
      return false;
    }
    if (reviewsList.size() < 3) {
      return false;
    }
    for (ReviewAssignment review : reviewsList) {
      if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    Grade firstGrade = reviewsList.get(0).getGrade();
    for (ReviewAssignment review : reviewsList) {
      if (review.getGrade() != firstGrade) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
}
